> The published tables, under the approved spec (kb/TABLES.md), since the switch digest labelled as a change of method. The frozen 2026-09-22 tables (`verity_numerical.bench.tables`) remain as a drill-down beside this render (`<stamp>-tables-frozen.md`).

Rules: `spec` (whole-proof bound 2^-128 or better).

Statement: the full relation commits x, W and y with the configuration's scheme and proves y = S(x, W) over the committed values; its scheme must be defined in verity.commitments (reason S otherwise). A configuration whose soundness argument uses an algebraic hash (Poseidon2 and the like) is admissible and flagged: algebraic hash, not for highest-stakes use. Bounds are the accountant's whole-proof figures at each cell's batching (`security.achieved_log2`), from proven proximity bounds only.

## Table 2 — End-to-end overhead per subcircuit, hardware class and commitment scheme

N = compute-bound datasheet estimate, instances/s (busiest unit, peak entry); P = instances/s end to end at the reference network (same datacenter, 1 ms RTT, 100 Gb/s: t = compute + rounds × RTT + bytes ÷ bandwidth) (committing the batch, `commit.seconds`, then proving); cell = N / P of the family's best admissible configuration (highest P). `(prov.)` = no red-team clearance of the statement yet; `(under re-verification)` = the latest `finding` label on the result is open (a `PULLED` one rejects it, D); `(alg.)` = its soundness argument uses an algebraic hash: algebraic hash, not for highest-stakes use (Table 1's flag); `—` = no admissible result; `n/s` = declared unsupported.

## Table 3 — Where the overhead comes from (each stage × N, share of the cell)

Each stage is its contribution to the Table 2 overhead: its time per instance × N, so commitment + interaction + the six buckets + Other add up to the cell (Total). Commitment is the serving overhead; interaction is the network wait at the reference profile (rounds × 1 ms + bytes ÷ 100 Gb/s, plus verifier compute the prover waits on); the buckets and Other are the proving overhead. A fused stage prints once in its first column, `↞` in the others. Setup, one-time (keys, circuit setup: `fixed.setup_seconds`) is outside the cell: its seconds, and ×native amortized over the measured batch (setup × N ÷ the batch's instances). Seconds per instance are in the drill-down below.

| Cell | Commitment | Interaction | Witness | Encoding + witness commitment | Arithmetic | Lookup | ZK additional | Serialization | Other | Total (the cell) | Setup, one-time (not in the total) |
